- **10:54 PDT** — Proposed a checkpoint-level red-team sidecar for online elicitation instead of a second feedback-driven solver trajectory. At each eligible checkpoint, a fixed-budget adversary would create one isolated white-box variant from the current public artifact and active rubric; the next proposer would receive the observed/adversarially-prompted pair with generation-role labels, while sidecar artifacts remain excluded from outcomes.
- **12:02 PDT** — Implemented a fourth `online_red_team` rubric arm with one isolated white-box sidecar per eligible checkpoint. - **12:08 PDT** — Rejected shadow evaluation as the primary remedy for rubric overfitting because it only diagnoses proposer failures. - **12:58 PDT** — Refined the proposer redesign to three stateless typed stages: linear artifact-level failure discovery with matched labeled contrasts, complete candidate-rubric design, and counterexample audit plus final editing. - **12:09 PDT** — Clarified that baseline-generated and observed artifacts are not clean ground truth, so candidate testing cannot require them to pass. - **21:18 PDT** — Refined multi-view assessment to preserve disagreement rather than require consensus. - **21:33 PDT** — Added a latest initial-to-current anchor to the proposed online evidence set. - **22:02 PDT** — Implemented blind criterion application without changing the validator model.
